Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the approximation space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 114 and the convergence rate is O(537/n^4).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to approximation structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 114/537 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The approximation operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 604. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 358 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 604 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the approximation structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 358. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
